# TASKS DONE

## Deduplicate Task And Commit Shared Procedures

Apply the three cut classes from `requirements.md` to `shared/complete-procedure.md`, `shared/commit-procedure.md`, `shared/task-format.md`, and `shared/get-current-milestone.md`: retire each `## Rules` section, relocating every surviving unique rule into the step where it acts or, when it constrains the whole file, into the opening description paragraph as prose; move editor-facing rationale and editor-addressed boundary paragraphs (such as `commit-procedure.md`'s `## The layer rule`) into the matching CLAUDE.md invariant by append-or-extend only, then cut them; and trim cross-file narration of the wrappers to the one sentence the contract needs, cutting a sentence only when its content survives earlier in the same file, in a referenced shared procedure, or in a CLAUDE.md invariant. Edit the files directly with ordinary editing tools rather than the skill-creator skill, and keep every point-of-use constraint, worked example, and once-rendered template (this shared-first ordering is what lets later wrapper cuts cite these files as survival homes). Verified by a per-file constraint-preservation ledger in this task's `TASKS_DONE.md` entry — one `**Verified:**` bullet per imperative removed or relocated, naming where it now survives (its point of use in the same file, the referenced shared procedure, or the CLAUDE.md invariant) — together with each file's `wc -w` word removal reported per cut class.

**Verified:**

- `shared/complete-procedure.md` — class A: the rule "Always follow the conventions from `CLAUDE.md` — never skip the project's way of verifying the work" survives at its point of use in step 3 ("follow the finishing conventions documented in `CLAUDE.md` … apply the project's way of verifying the change") and step 4's done-verification-convention paragraph.
- `shared/complete-procedure.md` — class A: "Never treat a task as done until every criterion of the derived bar is confirmed" survives at its point of use as step 4's closing line "Do not proceed to step 5 until every criterion passes."
- `shared/complete-procedure.md` — class A: "Never move a task to `TASKS_DONE.md` until step 4 passes completely, and never move it without the `**Verified:**` bullet list" survives at its point of use in step 4's closing line plus step 5's move-plus-augment instruction and its mandatory `**Verified:**` template.
- `shared/complete-procedure.md` — class A: "Never carry out a task that is not present in `<MILESTONE_DIR>/TASKS_TODO.md`" survives at its point of use in step 1 ("Do not carry out anything that is not a task in `TASKS_TODO.md`.").
- `shared/complete-procedure.md` — class A: "If a step fails, diagnose the root cause … do not skip steps or mark partial work as done" constrains the whole file rather than one step, so it was relocated verbatim in force into the opening description paragraph as prose, per the *Step-less rule placement* decision.
- `shared/complete-procedure.md` — class B: step 1's "Never use a hardcoded task-list path." survives in the CLAUDE.md invariant "Every runner that reads or writes a milestone's task list resolves `<MILESTONE_DIR>` by following `shared/get-current-milestone.md` … none may use a hardcoded task-list path."
- `shared/complete-procedure.md` — class B: step 3's "Recording paths as you go is neither committing nor content inspection." survives in the CLAUDE.md committing invariant ("staged path-scoped by explicitly named paths … no content inspection to choose the set"; "dispatched agents never commit"). The record-the-paths imperative itself stays at its point of use in step 3.
- `shared/complete-procedure.md` — class C: the two-bullet inline/isolation wrapper narration and "it says nothing about how the outcome is signalled" were trimmed to one contract sentence; their content survives in the CLAUDE.md invariants "`complete-task` exists as both a user-facing skill and an agent, differing only in *where* the shared procedure runs" and "The shared file is execution-neutral — it must not mention the `DONE`/`FAILED` return protocol, committing, or follow-up."
- `shared/complete-procedure.md` — class C: step 3's "a wrapper reads it from here when it needs to know exactly which files this task touched" survives in the CLAUDE.md orchestrator-commit-granularity clause ("path-scoped to the agent's handed-back change set plus the two task-list files").
- `shared/commit-procedure.md` — class A: "Stage only the given PATHS, explicitly named — never `git add -A`, never a tree-wide or content-driven selection" survives at its point of use in step 2.
- `shared/commit-procedure.md` — class A: "The path set is decided by the caller without content inspection; the no-op guard's status check is scoped to the already-given paths" survives at its point of use in the Inputs PATHS bullet and step 1's parenthetical ("*not* content inspection to decide the path set; the set is already given").
- `shared/commit-procedure.md` — class A: "On a no-op pass stage nothing and commit nothing — no `--allow-empty` empty commit" survives at its point of use in step 1.

- `shared/commit-procedure.md` — class A: "This file holds only the commit logic. ## Deduplicate Question Shared Procedures

Apply the three cut classes from `requirements.md` to `shared/answer-procedure.md`, `shared/answer-with-recommendation-procedure.md`, and `shared/recommend-procedure.md`: retire each `## Rules` section, relocating every surviving unique rule into the step where it acts or, when it constrains the whole file, into the opening description paragraph as prose; move editor-facing rationale and editor-addressed boundary paragraphs into the matching CLAUDE.md invariant by append-or-extend only, then cut them; and trim cross-file narration of the wrappers to the one sentence the contract needs, cutting a sentence only when its content survives earlier in the same file, in a referenced shared procedure, or in a CLAUDE.md invariant. Edit the files directly with ordinary editing tools rather than the skill-creator skill, keeping every point-of-use constraint, worked example, and once-rendered template, and leaving the SHORT TITLE / ANSWER input contracts and the principle-store fixed path untouched. Verified by a per-file constraint-preservation ledger in this task's `TASKS_DONE.md` entry — one `**Verified:**` bullet per imperative removed or relocated, naming where it now survives (its point of use in the same file, the referenced shared procedure, or the CLAUDE.md invariant) — together with each file's `wc -w` word removal reported per cut class.

**Verified:**

- `shared/answer-procedure.md` — class A: "Title matching is case-insensitive." survives at its point of use in step 2 ("case-fold both that un-escaped `id` and SHORT TITLE and compare") and in the Inputs SHORT TITLE bullet.
- `shared/answer-procedure.md` — class A: "The folded decision is clean prose with no citation marker — never annotate it with its provenance." survives at its point of use in step 5 ("Write it as **clean prose with no citation marker**: the document records the decision itself, not where it came from.").
- `shared/answer-procedure.md` — class A: "Do not rewrite or restructure existing content — only remove the answered entry (and any it moots) and add the decisions they produce." was relocated in force into the closing steps 4–6 sentence, where it acts ("…and do not otherwise rewrite or restructure existing content: only remove the answered entry and any it moots, and add the decisions they produce.").
- `shared/answer-procedure.md` — class A: "Do not invent implications not directly supported by the answer text." was relocated into step 3, the analysis step it constrains ("Do not invent implications the answer text does not directly support").
- `shared/answer-procedure.md` — class A: "If the answer is ambiguous or incomplete, remove what is clearly resolved and surface the rest rather than guessing — never add a brand-new question block to the document." was relocated verbatim in force into step 3.
- `shared/answer-procedure.md` — class B: step 2's "(This preserves the existing case-insensitive locate contract with zero regression.)" survives in the CLAUDE.md invariant "Step 2's locating keys off the block's `id` attribute (case-folded, entity-unescaped, matched by attribute-name-anchored regex so attribute order is immaterial)"; the case-insensitive locate itself stays at its point of use in step 2.
- `shared/answer-procedure.md` — class B: step 2's "Open and deferred blocks share that same pair (they differ only in the `status` attribute value), so the locate is uniform with no type-specific branch." was trimmed to "shared by open and deferred blocks alike"; the open/deferred coverage survives earlier in the same file (the Inputs bullet's "whether `status="open"` or `status="deferred"`") and the no-branch rationale in the CLAUDE.md invariant "This covers both cases uniformly: a bare block with no embedded children shares the one `<open-question …>`/`</open-question>` boundary-token pair and is removed exactly the same way."
- `shared/answer-procedure.md` — class B: step 4's four-line uniformity paragraph was cut to one sentence; the runner-facing imperative ("the same opening-through-closing removal clears the whole block whether or not it carries embedded `<alternative>` / `<applied-principle>` / `<recommendation>` children") stays at its point of use, and the cut rationale survives in the same CLAUDE.md step-4 invariant.
- `shared/answer-procedure.md` — class C: the two wrapper bullets (inline `answer-open-question` / by an orchestrator) and the wrapper-framing paragraph were trimmed to one contract sentence; the execution-neutral boundary survives in the CLAUDE.md invariant "That shared file is **execution-neutral** — it takes a resolved Short Title + answer and never mentions arg-parsing, committing, or the capture chain."
- `shared/answer-procedure.md` — class C: "Locating the block by its Short Title and cascading to mooted entries are shared work here; obtaining the title and answer is the caller's job." survives earlier in the same file, in the retained header sentence ("The caller supplies the two inputs below and wraps the result") and the Inputs SHORT TITLE bullet ("The caller has already obtained it; locating the matching block is this procedure's job").
- `shared/answer-with-recommendation-procedure.md` — class A: "Title matching is case-insensitive (case-fold both the un-escaped `id` and SHORT TITLE)." survives at its point of use in step 2 ("Locate the block whose `id` case-folds equal to SHORT TITLE … entity-unescaped, and case-folded against SHORT TITLE").
- `shared/answer-with-recommendation-procedure.md` — class A: "Derive ANSWER only from the block's `<recommendation>` element … never from any other element (in particular never from a sibling `<applied-principle>`, which keeps the answer provenance-free by construction), and never invent answer text." was relocated in force into step 3, the lift step it constrains; the `"<option>` — `<rationale>`" recombination it also stated already stood at its point of use in step 3's second paragraph.
- `shared/answer-with-recommendation-procedure.md` — class A: "The no-`<recommendation>`-element guard is a clean stop: … change nothing and record nothing." survives at its point of use in step 2's guard ("**stop without changing anything** and report why: nothing is recorded").
- `shared/answer-with-recommendation-procedure.md` — class A: "Do not restate `shared/answer-procedure.md`'s locate/analyse/remove/fold/cascade steps — reference it and let it own the recording; this file's job is only lift-then-delegate." survives in the CLAUDE.md invariant "`shared/answer-with-recommendation-procedure.md` is the single source of truth for the 'lift the embedded recommendation, then record it' logic, referenced (never restated) … It **composes over** the recording core"; the runner-facing half stays at its point of use in step 4 ("That procedure owns the recording work (locate, analyse, remove, fold, cascade); this procedure only lifts and delegates.").
- `shared/answer-with-recommendation-procedure.md` — class B: "This mirrors `shared/answer-procedure.md`'s clean stop on a Short-Title mismatch: nothing is recorded." was trimmed to "nothing is recorded"; the mirrored-guard rationale survives in the CLAUDE.md clause "with a **no-`<recommendation>`-element guard** that stops without changes when the recommend sweep never annotated the block".
- `shared/answer-with-recommendation-procedure.md` — class B: "This locating is a **read** to derive the answer — it is not the recording core's own locate/remove step, which runs later inside the delegated procedure." survives in the CLAUDE.md invariant's locate→lift→delegate composition ("given a resolved SHORT TITLE it locates the `<open-question>` block via the boundary-line CLI, lifts its `<recommendation option="...">…</recommendation>` element … then delegates to `shared/answer-procedure.md` with the derived ANSWER") and in this file's own step 4 delegation.
- `shared/answer-with-recommendation-procedure.md` — class C: the two wrapper bullets (inline skill / in-isolation agent) and the wrapper-framing paragraph were trimmed to one contract sentence; the skill-vs-agent framing survives in the CLAUDE.md invariant "The `answer-open-question-with-recommendation` skill + agent follow the established skill+agent pattern (one execution-neutral shared procedure, run **inline** by the skill so context survives, **in isolation** by the agent with the `DONE`/`FAILED` return protocol)".
- `shared/answer-with-recommendation-procedure.md` — class C: step 2's near-verbatim restatement of the recording core's locate spec (boundary-line CLI paragraph plus the `id`-regex / five-entity-unescape / case-fold / block-span paragraph) was replaced by a one-sentence reference; every clause survives in the referenced shared procedure, `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/shared/answer-procedure.md` step 2, which the new sentence names explicitly. The lift step's own five-predefined-entity substitution list (a distinct point of use, over `option` and the rationale text) is untouched.
- `shared/recommend-procedure.md` — class A: "Ground in the real project state before forming a view — the live project over memory — and read the project-wide principle store `milestones/answer_decision_principles.md` in place as part of that grounding." survives at its point of use in step 1's first two paragraphs, with the fixed `milestones/`-root path left exactly as it stood.
- `shared/recommend-procedure.md` — class A: "Two to four alternatives, no strawmen and no padding; each carrying what-it-is / key advantage / key drawback." survives at its point of use in step 2, including its three-item bullet list.
- `shared/recommend-procedure.md` — class A: "Exactly one recommendation, stated directly with no hedging; on a genuine tie, say so and name what breaks it." survives at its point of use in step 3's first paragraph.
- `shared/recommend-procedure.md` — class A: "A confirmed principle that bears on the question is a weighted advisory default … overridable only for a stated reason, never a veto that drops a candidate — and any principle that influenced the pick must be cited. When no principle bears, the recommendation is produced exactly as before." survives at its point of use in step 3's second paragraph.
- `shared/recommend-procedure.md` — class A: "Isolation is a cross-question constraint on the caller: never build one question's recommendation on another's. It never restricts the read-only grounding you do for the question at hand." survives at its point of use in step 1's third paragraph ("never treat another question's recommendation as an input to this one") and in the CLAUDE.md **Recommendation independence** invariant.
- `shared/recommend-procedure.md` — class A: "Produce only the alternatives and the recommendation as one contiguous, self-contained unit — nothing about how the result is rendered, recorded, or committed." survives in the same file's head paragraph ("The caller supplies the one input below and renders the result; this file describes only the analytical work itself", `:6-8`) and at its point of use in step 3's closing paragraph ("The alternatives and the recommendation together form one contiguous, self-contained unit that stays attached to the question it answers … not scattered commentary.", `:65-67`), plus the `CLAUDE.md` invariant calling the file "**execution-neutral** and holds only logical content". (Repointed by the *Repoint Three Stale Ledger Citations* task: the two homes this bullet originally cited — the Inputs scope line and the closing paragraph's wrapper-concern sentence — were themselves cut later, as residuals, by *Clear Four Residual Restatements From Layer* and *Resolve Four Remaining Layer Residuals*.)
- `shared/recommend-procedure.md` — class B: step 1's "(name that path directly; the confirmed principles are not a caller-supplied input alongside QUESTION)" survives near-verbatim in the CLAUDE.md invariant "(naming that fixed `milestones/`-root path directly — the confirmed principles are not a caller-supplied input alongside QUESTION)"; the imperative to read the store in place stays at its point of use.
- `shared/recommend-procedure.md` — class B: step 1's forward pointer "How a bearing principle shapes the recommendation is covered in step 3." carried no imperative of its own; its content is step 3's principle paragraph, still present in the same file, and the CLAUDE.md principle-aware invariant.
- `shared/recommend-procedure.md` — class B: "That isolation is a constraint the caller enforces across questions; it never narrows the honest grounding you do for the question at hand." survives in the CLAUDE.md **Recommendation independence** invariant ("'isolation' constrains the **orchestrator** … and does **not** narrow the subagent's read-only grounding … What the subagent must never do is treat another question's recommendation as an input.").
- `shared/recommend-procedure.md` — class C: the two wrapper bullets (`discuss-open-question` inline / the `recommend-open-question` read-only subagent) and the wrapper-framing paragraph were trimmed to one contract sentence naming both runners; the wrapper-ownership boundary survives in the CLAUDE.md invariant "the boundary is deliberate: `discuss-open-question` owns the WWCYM layer, the conversational framing, and the on-decision follow-up offers; the `recommend-open-question` agent owns the literal XML sub-element rendering".

## Deduplicate The Three Agent Files

Apply the three cut classes from `requirements.md` to `agents/complete-task.md`, `agents/recommend-open-question.md`, and `agents/answer-open-question-with-recommendation.md`: retire the recommend agent's `## Rules` section, relocating every surviving unique rule into the step where it acts or into the opening description paragraph as prose; move editor-facing rationale (such as the mutation-in-agent divergence) into the matching CLAUDE.md invariant by append-or-extend only, then cut it; trim narration of the orchestrators and sibling agents to the one sentence the contract needs; and render the `<open-question>` sub-element XML template exactly once in the recommend agent, with step 4 referring back to it, cutting a sentence only when its content survives earlier in the same file, in a referenced shared procedure, or in a CLAUDE.md invariant. Edit the files directly with ordinary editing tools rather than the skill-creator skill, keep every point-of-use constraint and the `DONE`/`FAILED` return protocols, and touch a frontmatter description only on a confirmed false claim against the edited body, adding no prose and removing no invocation vocabulary. Verified by a per-file constraint-preservation ledger in this task's `TASKS_DONE.md` entry — one `**Verified:**` bullet per imperative removed or relocated, naming where it now survives (its point of use in the same file, the referenced shared procedure, or the CLAUDE.md invariant) — together with each file's `wc -w` word removal reported per cut class.

**Verified:**

- `agents/recommend-open-question.md` — class A: rule 1 ("Read-only — mutate nothing… The orchestrator owns all document mutation, the embedding, and the staging; your job ends at handing back the sub-elements.") survives at its point of use in the opening paragraph ("**You read and reason; you never write** — never edit `requirements.md` or any other file"; "the orchestrator … owns everything you don't: it gathers the questions, embeds your returned sub-elements inside the existing `<open-question>` block, and stages the edit") and in step 4 ("Your only output is the XML sub-elements the orchestrator embeds").
- `agents/recommend-open-question.md` — class A: rule 2 ("Follow the shared procedure for the analytical core; do not restate it. This file owns only the XML rendering and the return protocol") survives at its point of use in step 2's reference sentence, and its editor-facing half survives in the CLAUDE.md invariant "`shared/recommend-procedure.md` … referenced (never restated) by both the `discuss-open-question` skill and the `recommend-open-question` agent" plus "the `recommend-open-question` agent owns the literal XML sub-element rendering".
- `agents/recommend-open-question.md` — class A: rule 3 ("Isolation is a cross-question constraint on the orchestrator, not on your grounding. Never treat another question's recommendation as an input") survives at its point of use in step 1's second paragraph and in the CLAUDE.md **Recommendation independence** invariant.
- `agents/recommend-open-question.md` — class A: rule 4 (keep the `<recommendation>` element well-formed for the lift — exactly one element whose `option` names the winning `<alternative id>` and whose text is the rationale alone) survives at its point of use in step 3's fourth bullet.
- `agents/recommend-open-question.md` — class A: rule 5 (one `<applied-principle>` element per bearing principle, sibling of `<recommendation>` and never its child, none when none bears, never folded into the `<recommendation>` text) survives at its point of use in step 3's second and third bullets.
- `agents/recommend-open-question.md` — class A: rule 6 (entity-escape with the five predefined XML entities; indent children 2 spaces per nesting level relative to the block's base column) survives at its point of use in step 3's fifth bullet (escaping) and step 3's opening paragraph (the 2-space-per-level indent relative to the base column).

Edit the files directly with ordinary editing tools rather than the skill-creator skill, keep every point-of-use constraint, worked example, and once-rendered template, and touch a frontmatter description only on a confirmed false claim against the edited body, adding no prose and removing no invocation or use-when vocabulary. ## Deduplicate Question Sweep Orchestrators

Apply the three cut classes from `requirements.md` to `skills/recommend-all-open-questions/SKILL.md` and `skills/answer-all-open-questions-with-recommendation/SKILL.md`: retire each `## Rules` section, relocating every surviving unique rule into the step where it acts or, when it constrains the whole skill, into the opening description paragraph as prose; move editor-facing rationale (such as the serialized-dispatch and orchestrator-commits justifications) into the matching CLAUDE.md invariant by append-or-extend only, then cut it; trim the narration of the dispatched agents and the shared procedures to the one sentence the contract needs; and split reference tails and terse-reporting justifications by audience, keeping every runner-facing owns-X clause, fixed success line, must-not-print list, and no-op branch. Edit the files directly with ordinary editing tools rather than the skill-creator skill, keep every point-of-use constraint, worked example, and once-rendered template (including the whole-block-replacement embedding shape), and touch a frontmatter description only on a confirmed false claim against the edited body, adding no prose and removing no invocation or use-when vocabulary. - `wc -w` word removal, per file: `skills/recommend-all-open-questions/SKILL.md` 1,778 → 1,258 (−520); `skills/answer-all-open-questions-with-recommendation/SKILL.md` 1,691 → 1,128 (−563). Two-file total 3,469 → 2,386 (−1,083).

## Deduplicate Milestone Definition Skills

Apply the three cut classes from `requirements.md` to `skills/init-milestone-base-workflow/SKILL.md`, `skills/discuss-milestone-goal/SKILL.md`, `skills/define-milestone-goal/SKILL.md`, `skills/specify-milestone-starting-state/SKILL.md`, and `skills/modify-milestone-goal/SKILL.md`: retire each `## Rules` section, relocating every surviving unique rule into the step where it acts or, when it constrains the whole skill (such as "Do not create any files" and "Do not update CLAUDE.md or milestones/README.md"), into the opening description paragraph as prose; move editor-facing rationale into the matching CLAUDE.md invariant by append-or-extend only, then cut it; and split reference tails and terse-reporting justifications by audience, keeping every runner-facing owns-X clause, fixed success line, must-not-print list, and no-op branch, and leaving the non-committing exemption of `init-milestone-base-workflow` intact. Edit the files directly with ordinary editing tools rather than the skill-creator skill, keep every point-of-use constraint, worked example, and once-rendered template, and touch a frontmatter description only on a confirmed false claim against the edited body, adding no prose and removing no invocation or use-when vocabulary. Verified by a per-file constraint-preservation ledger in this task's `TASKS_DONE.md` entry — one `**Verified:**` bullet per imperative removed or relocated, naming where it now survives (its point of use in the same file, the referenced shared procedure, or the CLAUDE.md invariant) — together with each file's `wc -w` word removal reported per cut class.

**Verified:**

- No `## Rules` section remains in any of the five files (`grep -n "^## Rules"` over all five returns nothing), and every `## Usage` block, worked example, and once-rendered template (`init`'s `milestones/README.md` and `CLAUDE.md` templates, `define`'s `requirements.md`/`TASKS_TODO.md`/`TASKS_DONE.md` templates and its number/slug worked examples, `discuss`'s **Understood goal:**/**Questions:** and **Proposed milestone goal:** output shapes, `specify`'s `## Relevant starting state` subsection template) is untouched.
- `init-milestone-base-workflow` — rule "Run once per project. If the project is already initialized (presence of `milestones/` and `milestones/README.md` with a `Current milestone:` line is sufficient), stop and report — do not re-scaffold" cut: survives at its point of use in step 1, whose already-initialized test names the same three conditions and ends "report that and stop without changing anything", and in the opening paragraph's "Run this **once** per project".
- `init-milestone-base-workflow` — rule "Never overwrite or rewrite an existing `milestones/README.md` or `CLAUDE.md` — only create missing files and append missing sections" cut: survives earlier in the same file in the opening idempotency paragraph ("creates missing scaffolding and inserts missing sections into existing files, but never overwrites or rewrites content that is already there") and at its two points of use, step 3 ("do not overwrite it … leave the rest of the file untouched") and step 4 ("update it without disturbing existing content" / "leave it exactly as-is — do not rewrite or re-template it"), and in the CLAUDE.md `init-milestone-base-workflow` invariant.
- `init-milestone-base-workflow` — rule "The current-milestone pointer lives only in `milestones/README.md`; do not write a pointer into `CLAUDE.md`" relocated to its point of use, the closing sentence of step 4 ("Never write a current-milestone pointer into `CLAUDE.md`; the pointer lives only in `milestones/README.md`"), which is the step that writes `CLAUDE.md`.
- `init-milestone-base-workflow` — rule "Do not create any `milestone_<N>_<slug>/` directory — that is `/define-milestone-goal`'s job" relocated to its point of use, step 2 ("Create no `milestone_<N>_<slug>/` directory inside it — that is `/define-milestone-goal`'s job"), the step that creates `milestones/`.
- `init-milestone-base-workflow` — rule "Do not document the project's environment context in `CLAUDE.md` — that is `/init`'s job; only recommend it" relocated to its point of use, the closing sentence of step 4 ("Never document the project's environment context in `CLAUDE.md` yourself either — recommending `/init` is as far as this skill goes"), adjacent to the `/init` recommendation it constrains; step 5's `/init` next-step suggestion is unchanged.
- `init-milestone-base-workflow` — rule "Do not commit — leave staging to the user" relocated as whole-skill prose into the opening description paragraph ("It does not commit — staging is left to the user"), per the *Step-less rule placement* decision; the non-committing exemption is intact both there and in the CLAUDE.md commit invariant's "Two exemptions" clause, and no commit step was added.
- `discuss-milestone-goal` — rules "Do not create any files. - `discuss-milestone-goal` — rule "Ask questions one at a time when the user seems overwhelmed; batch them when context is thin and answers are likely short" relocated verbatim into step 4 ("Continue the conversation"), the step that governs question pacing.
- `discuss-milestone-goal` — rule "Make a real judgment about what matters: do not list every possible edge case as a question" cut: survives earlier in the same file at step 3 ("Do not pad with preamble or list every possible concern — focus on the two or three questions whose answers would most change the scope") and step 4 ("Do not ask questions for their own sake").
- `discuss-milestone-goal` — rule "Keep each response short after the opening; the conversation should move fast" relocated into step 4 ("Keep each response short after the opening — the conversation should move fast").
- `define-milestone-goal` — rules "Do not update `CLAUDE.md` or `milestones/README.md` — those are only updated when the milestone becomes the *current* active milestone via `/goto-next-milestone`" and "Do not assign the milestone as current — defining a milestone does not activate it" relocated as one whole-skill prose sentence in the opening description paragraph ("Defining a milestone does not activate it: this skill never updates `CLAUDE.md` or `milestones/README.md`, which change only when the milestone becomes the *current* active one via `/goto-next-milestone`"), per the *Step-less rule placement* decision; both imperatives and the `/goto-next-milestone` pointer survive.

- `define-milestone-goal` — rule "Never overwrite an existing milestone directory" cut: survives at its point of use in step 3 ("Verify that `milestones/milestone_<NN>_<slug>/` does not already exist. If a milestone with that number exists under any slug, stop and report the conflict"), which is the gate that prevents the overwrite.

## Run Fresh-Context Runtime Layer Re-Audit

In a fresh context, audit every `skills/*/SKILL.md`, `agents/*.md`, and `shared/*.md` file plus the sweep tasks' `**Verified:**` ledgers (checking each ledger claim against the live file, with git history supplying the original text) and report findings without fixing anything, classifying each as a blocking lost constraint (an imperative removed with no surviving home, or a ledger claim that does not hold) or a residual restatement (a remaining `## Rules` heading, editor-facing rationale still in a runtime file, or cross-file narration beyond the one contract sentence). Exclude residuals already registered with a reason in `temp/milestone_15_findings.md`, and return a clean verdict only when there are zero loss findings and no unregistered residuals; findings that are not clean become follow-up fix tasks queued ahead of the two tasks below, followed by a further whole-layer re-audit, repeating until a run is clean. Verified by the classified findings list and verdict recorded in this task's `TASKS_DONE.md` entry.

**Verified:**

- All 32 runtime files (22 `skills/*/SKILL.md`, 3 `agents/*.md`, 7 `shared/*.md`) were audited in fresh contexts by seven independent read-only auditors, none carrying the sweep's editing context, over disjoint file groups covering the layer exactly once, each given the milestone's three cut classes, the cut rule, and `git diff 3ca3234..HEAD` as the record of what each file lost.
- All 13 completed sweep tasks' `**Verified:**` ledgers were checked claim by claim against the live files, with `git show 3ca3234:<path>` supplying the original text each claim is about and each named survival home (point of use, referenced shared procedure, or `CLAUDE.md` invariant) opened and confirmed in force rather than merely topically adjacent.
- Nothing was fixed: `git status --porcelain` reported a clean tree throughout, and this task modified no file under `skills/`, `agents/`, `shared/`, nor `CLAUDE.md` or `README.md`.
- Every finding is classified as a blocking lost constraint or a residual restatement, per the milestone's *Verification* decision.
- The registered-residual exclusion was applied and excluded nothing: `temp/milestone_15_findings.md` does not exist, so every residual found counts as unregistered.
- LOSS 1 (blocking, ledger claim that does not hold) — `TASKS_DONE.md:511`, "Verify README Claims Against Swept Layer": the scope line claims the pass read "all 33 runtime files (23 `skills/*/SKILL.md`, 3 `agents/*.md`, 7 `shared/*.md`)". The live layer is 32 files with 22 skills (`find skills -name SKILL.md | wc -l` = 22, identical at `3ca3234`, no skill directory added or deleted in the milestone), so the stated scope overcounts by one file. The same bullet's own change-set figure ("32 files, 309 insertions / 952 deletions") reproduces exactly, and no file appears to have been skipped; the miscount is inherited from `requirements.md`'s starting-state paragraph.
- LOSS 2 (blocking, ledger claim that does not hold) — `TASKS_DONE.md:531`, `## How skills commit` bullet: it certifies README's mechanics claim with "all 24 references to it remain". Only 16 remain — `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/shared/commit-procedure.md` occurrences across `skills/`, `agents/`, `shared/` fall from 24 at `3ca3234` to 16 at HEAD, the sweep having removed a duplicate second mention in seven skills. The README claim the bullet supports still holds (16 referencing files, one reference each, and steps 1–3 of `shared/commit-procedure.md` are still guard / staging / commit), so the defect is in the ledger's evidence, not in `README.md`.
- LOSS 3 (blocking, ledger claim that does not hold) — `TASKS_DONE.md:554`, `### ask-in-milestone-context` bullet: it lists among the README:309 claims it verified "the task→commit mapping is best-effort, not keyed on the heading in the subject line" and closes "with text matching what README already said". That sentence is not in `README.md` at all (`grep -c 'best-effort' README.md` = 0); it lives at `skills/ask-in-milestone-context/SKILL.md:53`. The bullet attributes a runtime-file sentence to `README.md`; its other claims about README:309 do hold.
- RESIDUAL 1 (unregistered, editor-facing rationale still in a runtime file) — `skills/migrate-workspace/SKILL.md:39-42` and `:52-54`: "**Each future milestone that changes a workspace artifact appends one entry here**; the workflow below applies whatever the catalog contains, so the procedure never changes — only this list grows" and "If a template later changes, update the matching catalog entry's `Rewrite to` so the destination tracks it" both address someone registering a future migration rather than the runner executing one, and neither changes what a `/migrate-workspace` run does. It is the survival home that entry's own class-B ledger bullet cited when cutting the file's `## Adding a future migration` section, so the content must be relocated to a `CLAUDE.md` invariant and the ledger bullet repointed, not simply deleted.
- Verdict: **not clean** — 3 loss findings and 1 unregistered residual, so the milestone's convergence condition (zero loss findings and no unregistered residuals) is not met.
- Follow-up fix tasks queued in `TASKS_TODO.md` ahead of the two remaining tasks, followed by a further whole-layer re-audit: "Correct Three False Ledger Claims", "Relocate Migrate-Workspace Maintainer Prose", then "Re-Audit Runtime Layer After Fixes", ordered above "Measure And Report Word-Count Removal" and "Extend Transpile Script And Regenerate Tree".
- Findings that were checked and did not survive as findings, recorded so the next re-audit does not re-raise them: no `## Rules` heading remains anywhere in the layer; no "do not restate those mechanics here" / "never restate them here" editor imperative remains; every `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/...` reference in the layer resolves to a file that exists; all 25 `SKILL.md`/agent files still carry `name` and `description` frontmatter; the duplicate `<open-question>` XML template in `agents/recommend-open-question.md` is now rendered exactly once; and the two known-false `README.md` claims about `goto-next-milestone` are pre-existing at `3ca3234`, not sweep-caused, and correctly left unedited under the *README sync scope* decision.

workspace artifact appends one entry here**; the workflow below applies whatever the catalog
contains, so the procedure never changes — only this list grows" and "If a template later changes,
update the matching catalog entry's `Rewrite to` so the destination tracks it" — into a matching
`CLAUDE.md` invariant first and then cut them from the runtime file, since both address someone
registering a future migration rather than the runner executing one and neither changes what a
`/migrate-workspace` run does. Because that same prose is the survival home the completed sweep's
ledger cited when cutting the file's `## Adding a future migration` section, the corresponding
`TASKS_DONE.md` bullet must be repointed at the new `CLAUDE.md` home in the same pass so no ledger
claim is falsified by the cut. Verified by confirming the two sentences are absent from the
`SKILL.md`, present in the `CLAUDE.md` invariant, and cited there by the repointed ledger bullet,
with the runner-facing "Treat those templates as the authority for the target state" instruction
and every catalog entry left intact.

**Verified:**

- Both are present in `CLAUDE.md` first, as one **appended** invariant bullet (the last bullet of `## Invariants to preserve when editing skills`, no existing bullet reshaped, merged, or removed — `git diff CLAUDE.md` is 1 insertion, 0 deletions): registration ("**each future milestone that changes a workspace artifact appends one entry to that catalog** — `Targets` / `Detect` / `Rewrite to` — and **leaves the workflow alone**, because the workflow applies whatever the catalog contains, so the procedure never changes; only this list grows") and template-tracking ("**if a template later changes, update the matching catalog entry's `Rewrite to` so the destination tracks it**"), both framed as maintainer acts the runner never needs.

Measure the actual word removal of the sweep with `wc -w` over `skills/`, `agents/`, and `shared/` against the 32,758-word baseline, and report it in total and per cut class (summing the per-class figures the deduplication tasks' ledgers recorded) as the honest outcome against the non-binding roughly 11,000-word target, treating any shortfall as information only and never as a trigger for further cuts. Verified by the total and per-class figures recorded in this task's `TASKS_DONE.md` entry alongside the baseline they are measured against.

**Verified:**

- Live layer measured with `wc -w skills/*/SKILL.md agents/*.md shared/*.md` over the same 32 files the baseline covers (22 `skills/*/SKILL.md`, 3 `agents/*.md`, 7 `shared/*.md`): **25,161 words**, against the milestone's **32,758-word baseline**, which reproduces exactly at the pre-sweep commit `3ca3234` by the same command. - The remaining **−233 words** fall outside the eleven deduplication ledgers, in the post-sweep residual-cleanup tasks, and are itemized here so the classes and the measured total reconcile: `Relocate Migrate-Workspace Maintainer Prose` −47 (class B, per its own ledger); `Clear Four Residual Restatements From Layer` −33; `Resolve Four Remaining Layer Residuals` −72 (its commit `b088180` carries 25,347 → 25,242, of which the preceding −33 was uncommitted at the time); `Clear Three Opening-Paragraph Restatements From Layer` −45; `Clear Capture Opening-Paragraph Reasoning Restatement` −16; and the two still-uncommitted working-tree cuts (`skills/migrate-workspace/SKILL.md` −3, `skills/recommend-all-open-questions/SKILL.md` −17) −20. 7,364 + 233 = 7,597, the measured total.
